Newton's Third Law
- For every force by object A to object B Caction), there is an equal but opposite force exerted by object B to object Acreaction).
-Reaction force is equal in magnitude but opposite in direction
- Forces always act in pairs
-an object cannot move itself
-action/reaction forces always act on different objects
